| (this change) | `api.denied` is capped at 60 events a minute in all, besides once a minute per caller and route. | [audit and governance](features/audit-and-governance.md), [limitations.md](limitations.md) |
| (this change) | A credential test whose plugin throws after the credential was sent records a `credential.test` failure (with the error's class name) before the generic 500. | [audit and governance](features/audit-and-governance.md) |
| (this change) | Outbound: remote MCP servers check the MCP list on every redirect hop; IPv4-compatible and 6to4 addresses are judged by their IPv4 part and `64:ff9b:1::/48` is reserved; an OpenAI-compatible refusal shows the Blocked message as the error; a test covers the SIEM `node:http` path with blocking on; the configuration row, `withGovernance`'s comment, the outbound docs and the not-covered list are current. | [audit and governance](features/audit-and-governance.md#outbound-requests), [limitations.md](limitations.md) |
| (this change) | Expressions: the `async` check swaps in an identifier with a Cyrillic letter, so a scope with both `async` and `asynk` is no longer refused; engine.md notes the realm's own `console`; development.md says how to run the engine suite on a newer Node and no longer hard-codes the highest migration. | [engine](features/engine.md), [development](development.md#verification) |
| (this change) | `statement-cache.test.ts` covers the cached write path (upsert, insertNew, delete) under a cap of two. | [development](development.md#verification) |
| (this change) | Roadmap: the wave 3 carryover applied in wave 4 and what was deferred, with the reasons; the backend hardening list is back in package order. | [roadmap.md](roadmap.md#backend-hardening-2026-11) |
| (this change) | The constitution, the Claude Code agents and the `landflow-feature` skill describe the code wave 4 built: the target marks of P16 to P18 are gone and the rules name `parseQuery` with `pageSchema`, keyset paging over `executions-query.ts`, the body cap and API rate limits, the configuration catalog and its generated page, `requestLogFields()` and the span attributes, wider redaction, `sseResponse()` and the store's execution-finished event. | [constitution](constitution.md) |
| (this change) | Roadmap: the wave 3 carryover's editor and expression live checks that are still open (saves after Spotlight, publish, restore and a lock takeover; a rejected promise under `next dev` and `next start`). | [roadmap.md](roadmap.md#backend-hardening-2026-11) |
| (this change) | Wave 4 review follow-ups. Audit details keep a number or boolean under a secret-looking key (the AI caps event lost its `credentials` count to P17's wider key rule). The SSE buffer limit is 16 MiB, not 1 MiB: a healthy reader rescheduled late was cut mid-run, and the run and assistant streams cannot resume. The live feed opens a new EventSource, backing off up to a minute, after a non-200 answer (429, 503, 401), which ends the browser's own retries. The logger also scrubs `sk-`, GitHub and Slack keys, and the route error line carries the request id once. deployment.md names the production rate limits and the body cap. | [observability](observability.md), [deployment](deployment.md), [limitations](limitations.md) |
| (this change) | Tests: `kagent-oci.test.ts` gets its own data directory. It deletes `oci/` while `kagent.test.ts` packs images into the shared one, which failed under full-suite load with ENOTEMPTY (the same cause as the marketplace flake in wave 3). | [development](development.md#verification) |
| (this change) | SSE: a byte limit alone cut healthy editor runs (live: three Code nodes of 3 MB each queued 15.6 MB in 0.4 s while the client read, and the stream ended at the fourth). `sseResponse` now cuts a client over 16 MiB only when it has read nothing for 10 s (`stallMs`), or any client over 128 MiB (`hardLimitBytes`, 8 × the limit). Tests cover a burst a reader catches up on, a stall, and the hard limit. | [api](reference/api.md), [limitations](limitations.md) |
| (this change) | P21, audit reliability. A failed audit write is counted (`landflow.audit.write_failures{action}`) and the outbox reports depth, oldest age and delivery failures. `LANDFLOW_AUDIT_FAIL_CLOSED=true` makes role changes, API key create and revoke, credential create, update and delete, audit settings and SCIM provisioning refuse with 503 when their event cannot be stored: the change and its event share one transaction, or (credentials, a file store) the event goes first. Off by default. | [audit-and-governance](features/audit-and-governance.md#reliability-and-fail-closed-mode) |
| (this change) | P22: saturation metrics (event-loop delay and utilisation, heap and RSS, GC pauses), `landflow.db.query.duration` for every database call with a `db.query` span only for calls over `LANDFLOW_DB_SLOW_MS` (and always for Postgres `direct()`), SSE connection, sign-in failure and limiter refusal counters. Read-only API requests are traced when slower than `LANDFLOW_TRACE_SLOW_READ_MS` or failed (5xx), fast ones are dropped with their child spans. Breaking for dashboards: `landflow.workflow.id` is off the schedule-lag histogram and, unless `LANDFLOW_METRICS_WORKFLOW_LABEL=true`, off three counters. | [observability](observability.md#5-metrics), [configuration](reference/configuration.md) |
| (this change) | P23, encryption key ring (S13). Sealed values carry a key id (`v2`, AES-GCM with the credential id or a purpose as associated data), `LANDFLOW_ENCRYPTION_KEYS` holds several keys (first seals), passphrase keys use scrypt (the unsalted SHA-256 form still opens old data as `k0-legacy`), `deriveKey` is HKDF per label (resume, approval and OAuth2 state verify under every ring key and the old HMAC, so links in flight survive), and `POST /api/settings/encryption/rotate` / `pnpm --filter @landflow/web rotate-key` re-seal credentials and every other sealed store, idempotently. | [secrets and environments](features/secrets-and-environments.md#encryption-keys-and-rotation) |
| (this change) | Sessions and sign-in hardening (P24). Changing a password ends the user's other sessions and keeps yours (`auth.password_change` gains `sessionsRevoked`); `LANDFLOW_SESSION_IDLE_HOURS` signs out idle sessions (72 in production, off in development); a cookie request with no Origin and a cross-site `Sec-Fetch-Site` is refused; `LANDFLOW_LOCAL_SIGN_IN` (`all`, `owner`, `off`) limits password sign-in (password reset and Forgot password follow it too) and the login page hides the form when it is off. TOTP is on the roadmap. | [authentication](features/authentication.md), [configuration](reference/configuration.md), [limitations](limitations.md) |
| (this change) | P25: an async read seam for run data. `db/reads.ts` wraps the reads of a run's saved record and journal in promises; `store.loadExecution`, `executionsMentioningAsync` and async journal readers sit on it, and the callers that may read a finished run (execution routes, run search/lines/compare/measures, needs-you, OTLP export, MCP tools, Slack, approval links, the brain, the binary sweep) now await them. `store.getExecution` is documented as runs in flight only and a source scan in `execution-reads.test.ts` keeps new callers off it. No behaviour change. | [architecture](architecture.md#storage), [roadmap](roadmap.md) |
| (this change) | Wave 5 integration. `audit-reliability.test.ts`'s fake meter gains `createHistogram` and `createUpDownCounter`: since P22 every database call records `landflow.db.query.duration`, so the host instruments are built on whatever meter the test installs. The static import closure of `http.ts` is re-measured after merging P21-P25: 86 files (P21's `audit/metrics.ts` and `config/audit.ts`, P25's `db/reads.ts` in); the budget follows (79 * 1.1 rounds up to 87). | [development](development.md) |
| (this change) | Sessions: the live event stream's 30 s re-check resolves the session without marking it used (`sessionUser`/`resolvePrincipal` take `touch: false`), so the stream alone no longer keeps a session alive past `LANDFLOW_SESSION_IDLE_HOURS`; its stream closes when the limit passes. (An open editor's lock heartbeat and the pages' polling still marked it used until the background-request row below.) A throw in the re-check closes the stream instead of reaching the process's uncaught-exception handler. | [authentication](features/authentication.md) |
| (this change) | Tidying from the wave 4 reviews: the store's in-flight comment sits on `IN_FLIGHT` again and the finished-execution payload is built once for every listener; `settings.ts` names `retentionTick` once; `zod` is imported first in the executions and usage routes; the generated configuration page ends a description with a period before its alias and "Also read by plugins" notes; `config-catalog.test.ts` puts the ratchet's comment back on the ratchet and says the scan covers only `apps/web/src`. | [configuration.md](reference/configuration.md) |
| (this change) | The effective configuration shows `LANDFLOW_SECRETS_CACHE_TTL` and `LANDFLOW_SECRETS_TIMEOUT_MS` as they are: a catalogued variable the catalog does not mark secret no longer falls to the secret-looking-name rule, which still hides every uncatalogued name. | [configuration.md](reference/configuration.md) |
| (this change) | The usage and AI usage retention variables are read through `envWithAliases` (`lib/server/config`), which takes the aliases from the catalog and treats an empty value as unset, so `LANDFLOW_USAGE_RETENTION_DAYS=` no longer hides a set `USAGE_RETENTION_DAYS`. `aiUsageRetentionDays()` replaces the source-text check in `singletons.test.ts` with a behaviour test; comments, the usage route's OpenAPI text, observability.md and second-brain.md name the new variables. Two fewer files read `process.env` (the ratchet is 86). | [development](development.md#conventions) |
| (this change) | `GET /api/executions`: a resumed run that is live and still saved as `waiting` is left out of later pages too, not only the first, so paging lists it once. | [reference/api.md](reference/api.md) |
| (this change) | The instance MCP server's `list_executions` with a `status` no longer reads every saved run: `listExecutionsPage` takes a `status` filter in SQL and the tool reads pages of 200 until it has its `limit` (at most ten pages). `status: running` lists the live runs only. | [reference/api.md](reference/api.md) |
| (this change) | API rate limits: a process counts at most 50,000 keys a window (a new key past that drops the oldest), so forged `X-Forwarded-For` values can't grow the map within a minute; the handler-level test freezes `Date` so its calls can't straddle a minute boundary. | [development](development.md#verification) |
| (this change) | Docs from the wave 4 reviews: architecture.md describes the bus's replay ring, `events-stream.ts` and `sse.ts`; development.md says the execution reactions keep the code of the graph that booted under `pnpm dev`; limitations.md says the replay ring is bounded by count, not bytes, and which Bearer tokens the log scrubber leaves; the P18 row names the `LANDFLOW_CODE_IN_PROCESS=false` change. `singletons.test.ts` resets the finished-execution listeners with the reactions. | [architecture.md](architecture.md) |
| (this change) | Roadmap and limitations: the wave 4 carryover applied in wave 5 and what was deferred, with the reasons; the public paths that no rate limit covers. | [roadmap.md](roadmap.md#backend-hardening-2026-11) |
| (this change) | The constitution, the Claude Code agents and the `landflow-feature` skill describe the code wave 5 built: audit events required through `auditedTransaction()` / `auditedChange()` and `LANDFLOW_AUDIT_FAIL_CLOSED`, the key ring (`key-ring.ts`, `deriveKey` / `deriveKeys`, `rotateEncryption()`), session limits and `touch: false` for background re-checks, `LANDFLOW_LOCAL_SIGN_IN` and the `Sec-Fetch-Site` check, the run-data read seam (`db/reads.ts`, `store.loadExecution`, the journal's async readers), database timing, slow-read tracing and the saturation metrics; the security reviewer's open findings drop S9, S13 and S14. P26 to P30 keep their marks. | [constitution](constitution.md) |
| (this change) | Sessions: requests the browser makes on its own no longer count as use. `api(path, { background: true })` sends `X-Landflow-Background: 1` from the edit-lock heartbeat, every polling load (executions pages, Run Trace, run controls, follow-run, agent sessions and their panel, the notebook, evaluations, poll status, debug tokens, workers and MCP settings) and their reloads after live events; `guardRequest()` resolves those and `GET /api/events` with `touch: false`, so an editor left open reaches `LANDFLOW_SESSION_IDLE_HOURS`. | [authentication](features/authentication.md), [constitution](constitution.md) |
| (this change) | `landflow.sse.connections` is labelled by route template: the five streaming routes name theirs (`/api/events`, `/api/workflows/:id/run`, `/api/executions/:id/approvals`, `/api/assistant/chat`, `/api/assistant/agent`) and a stream that names none counts as `other`. The path heuristic it replaces kept ids without a digit (about one in six) in the label, one series per run stream for the life of the process. | [observability](observability.md), [limitations](limitations.md) |
| (this change) | P22's three files back in the repo's style (single quotes, long lines): `db/database.ts`, `telemetry/processors.ts` and `test/read-sampling.test.ts` had been rewritten in Prettier defaults, so their diff held about 250 lines of quote and wrap churn. The logic of the last P22 fix is re-applied by hand (a read is freed once it and its spans have ended, at most 5,000 reads in flight, `direct()` ends its span on a synchronous throw), with new tests for a span started after its read was freed and for the 5,000-read cap. The limitations and observability pages now say what the code does: a finished read is not remembered, and a later span under its context is exported on its own. | [limitations.md](limitations.md#audit-reliability-and-observability-backend-hardening-2026-11) |
| (this change) | Wave 5 review notes: with only `LANDFLOW_ENCRYPTION_KEYS` set the production check no longer warns that the key file is used (catalog `satisfiedBy`); compose, queue compose, `.env.example` and the Kubernetes host, queue main and worker pass the key ring through (empty by default); a credential whose write fails during rotation keeps its stored seal in memory (test added); `LANDFLOW_SESSION_IDLE_HOURS` is read with `envNumber` (an invalid value is a config issue); the audit outbox gauges are held in `singleton()`; the docs say key rotation and password changes are fail-open on purpose, that a validation-refused credential change leaves an event in fail-closed mode, that a `v2` value can't be opened by an older build, and the forwarded-header item points at P27 only. | [deployment.md](deployment.md#encryption-key-rotation) |
| (this change) | P26, Postgres mode keeps only hot run data in memory: boot loads the data and journal of runs in flight or finished in the last `LANDFLOW_PG_HOT_MINUTES` (15) only, a minute timer evicts what cooled down without writing to Postgres, and the async read seam serves cold runs and scans from Postgres (one query, after a barrier on the write queue); deletes and retention sweeps on the partly mirrored tables are always replayed, a decision on an old run's check loads it back first, queue-mode followers keep cold rows empty, and `landflow.db.replication.lag` is a new gauge. With 50,000 finished runs (`apps/web/scripts/seed-executions.ts`, about 10 KB and 20 journal events each) the host booted in 0.7 s using 164 MB instead of 10.1 s and 14.1 GB. | [architecture.md](architecture.md#storage) |
| (this change) | P26 review fixes: the chat feedback, form waiting, run and execute duplicate, stop and approval handlers load a finished run on demand (`store.loadExecution`), so a run older than the hot window answers as before (409 with its status, the finished page, its real duplicate status) instead of 404 or `running`; promotions are held in a `singleton()`, `promoteExecution` keeps a still-hot run hot too, an automatic retry promotes its source before its `retries` row goes, a queue-mode reload takes one cutoff for all tables; tests for cold stop, form and approval answers and for the `executions` column list; limitations and observability say what `lastRunFields`, the team-id scan and clock skew do. | [limitations.md](limitations.md) |
| (this change) | P27: every domain error with a status extends `HttpError` and 84 route `catch` blocks that only mapped one are removed; responses carry a `code` as well; `projectRoute` and `dataTableRoute` are plain `handler()`; `test/error-mapping.test.ts` | [development](development.md#conventions) |
| (this change) | P28, boundaries as checks: `runner.ts` is split along its sections into `lib/server/runner/` (types, state, limits, persist, run, resume, rerun, queue, recovery, links, live, stop, jobs) behind a barrel, so its 44 importers and `import('../runner')` are unchanged, and `ResumeError` / `RerunError` extend `HttpError`; dependency-cruiser enforces the layering with `pnpm check:deps` (known cycles and two route-to-db imports kept in `.dependency-cruiser-known-violations.json`) and `pnpm check` runs typecheck, `pnpm test` and check:deps (no CI workflow); the `landflow-sync/v1` names, path rules and manifest type moved to `packages/protocol/src/sync-format.ts` (the CLI imports its `@landflow/protocol/sync-format` subpath, so its bundle stays 137 kB). | [development](development.md#checking-the-boundaries), [git sync](features/git-sync.md) |
| (this change) | Alert rules, a dashboard and exception fingerprints (backend hardening P29). Error log lines and exception span events carry `exception.fingerprint` (a hash of the type and the top in-repo frames without line numbers), so one bug groups across releases. `deploy/observability/prometheus-rules.yml` has 13 alerts (5xx ratio, p95, schedule lag, queue backlog, no workers, plugin offline, Postgres writes blocked and old, audit write failures and outbox age, event-loop delay, process errors, rate-limit refusals), each with a runbook link; `landflow-dashboard.json` is the overview dashboard. The observability overlay mounts both (with a Prometheus config that adds `rule_files`). A test checks every metric they name exists in the code. | [observability.md](observability.md#alerts-and-dashboard) |
| (this change) | P30: an optional strict backend for `{{ }}` expressions. `LANDFLOW_EXPRESSION_SANDBOX=isolate` runs them in an isolated-vm isolate with a memory cap (`LANDFLOW_EXPRESSION_MEMORY_MB`, default 64) that fails a memory bomb without ending the process, behind the same bootstrap and bridge as the sealed realm; `realm` stays the default and `isolated-vm` is an optional dependency of the engine (pinned to 6.x, Node 22). The image sets `NODE_OPTIONS=--no-node-snapshot` and links the addon; a production start that asks for `isolate` where it cannot load refuses to boot, development falls back to the realm | [engine](features/engine.md#the-isolate-backend-optional) |
| (this change) | P30 review fixes: the expression backend choice is kept on `globalThis` so every module graph (instrumentation, each route) sees it, where a module-level variable left route resolvers on the realm (the preview route is tested on the isolate); `Array.from` on a long array-like is guarded too (`Array.from({ length: 1e8 }, f)` aborted the process), each bomb is tested in a process of its own, and the docs say the guard list cannot be proven complete; contexts are released by an LRU of 32 instead of the host's garbage collector, the bootstrap is compiled once per isolate, the script cache follows the cap, and a disposal that is not the expression's fault rebuilds and retries once (a churn test of 5,000 resolvers has no failure; a fresh resolver is 1.3 times slower than the realm); the isolate suite now really runs the imported realm suite on the isolate (its backend was reset too early); an unknown `LANDFLOW_EXPRESSION_SANDBOX` refuses the production boot; wrapped builtins keep their `length` | [engine](features/engine.md#the-isolate-backend-optional) |
| (this change) | P30 second review fixes: the isolate docs now say what was measured, that hash-table growth (`Set`/`Map` loops, sparse object keys, `groupBy`) aborts the process at the default cap and that the cap does not bound resident memory (0.5 to 2 GB at 64 MB), so `isolate` is no defence against a memory bomb; a test pins the abort; the config catalog and generated page agree; the Alpine image is verified | [engine](features/engine.md#the-isolate-backend-optional) |
| (this change) | Wave 6 integration. P26's re-run, retry, run and webhook routes imported `promoteExecution` from `lib/server/db/reads.ts`, which P28's `routes-not-db` rule refuses: they call `store.promoteExecution` instead (a one-line delegate on the store). The static import closure of `http.ts` is re-measured after merging P26-P30: 89 files (P26's `db/hot-mirror.ts` and `db/replication-metrics.ts` through `db/database.ts`, P29's `telemetry/fingerprint.ts` through the logger, three small leaf modules); the budget follows (81 * 1.1 rounds up to 90). | [development](development.md#verification) |
| (this change) | Wave 5 carryover, applied in wave 6. Tests: `Database.atomic()` on its own (`database-atomic.test.ts`), fail-closed audit for API key revoke, credential update and delete, the audit settings PATCH and SCIM groups, one `landflow.auth.sign_in_failures` count per failed sign-in, the `rotate-key` script on Postgres (`rotate-key-postgres.test.ts`, `LANDFLOW_INTEGRATION=queue`), an anchoring save after a rotation keeping the new seal, Forgot password and the reset page under `LANDFLOW_LOCAL_SIGN_IN`, the login form rendered per mode (no longer a source scan), and a scan keeping the sync journal readers to runs in flight. Code: the audit outbox gauges register at boot even with the outbox off; `GET /api/auth/password/reset` answers 403 for a user the mode refuses, so the page says so before its form; the login page drops its welcome line when nobody can sign in; the unused sync `store.executionsMentioning` is removed; the brain's usage backfill is fire-and-forget. Measured: `landflow.db.query.duration` costs about 0.15 µs a call with the metrics SDK on. Docs: the audit instruments in the metrics table, clearing a value no ring key opens, why the local sign-in refusal follows the password, readiness and the outbox. Deferred (roadmap): a background rotation job, a hint on Users for refused reset links, a better CSP probe, and the live checks. | [roadmap](roadmap.md#backend-hardening-2026-11), [authentication](features/authentication.md), [observability](observability.md#5-metrics) |
| (this change) | The constitution, the `landflow-*` agents and the feature skill describe the code after the backend refactor's last wave: no rule is marked *target* any more. New or corrected: `HttpError` and the pass-through route wrappers (P27), the dependency rules and `pnpm check` as the local gate, the shared `landflow-sync/v1` layout and the runner's modules (P28), the hot mirror and `promoteExecution` before writing to an old run (P26), `exception.fingerprint` and shipping alert rules with a paging metric (P29), the isolate expression backend (P30); the security reviewer's open findings now point at the limitations that remain. | [constitution](constitution.md), [development](development.md#working-with-claude-code-constitution-agents-feature-skill) |
| (this change) | Wave 6 gate rerun after the carryover, and P26's measurement repeated: `scripts/seed-executions.ts` with 50,000 finished runs in the local `landflow_test` database boots the hot mirror in 0.7 s at 156 MB resident (1.3 s and 145 MB on tables bloated by re-seeding), against 9.3 s and 14.5 GB with every run hot (`LANDFLOW_PG_HOT_MINUTES=100000`, the behaviour before P26), which also needs `--max-old-space-size=16384`: at Node's default 4 GB heap that boot runs out of memory. The seeded rows were deleted afterwards. | [deployment](deployment.md#postgres-memory-sizing) |
| (this change) | Domain error codes survive the production build: `HttpError` took `code` and `name` from `new.target.name`, which `next build` minifies, so the image answered `{ error, code: "v" }` (a letter that changed per build) and the exception fingerprint of a domain error changed with every rebuild. Every `HttpError` subclass (63) now starts with a literal `static readonly errorName: string = 'ItsName'`, which `code` and `name` come from, and `test/error-mapping.test.ts` fails a subclass in `lib/server` or `app` without one; the transport errors (`@landflow/transports`) get literal names the same way. Checked on a production build: the queue stack's image answers a 404 from `POST /api/executions/:id/checks` with `code: "check_error"`. The wave 6 gate passed again, and the 50,000-run boot measured 0.5 s at 153 MB resident (9.6 s and 14.5 GB with every run hot); the seeded rows were deleted. | [constitution](constitution.md), [development](development.md) |
| (this change) | Wave 6 review leftovers. Hot mirror: a pass of the evictor leaves promoted runs out in SQL and goes on while a full chunk evicts something, so 500 or more promoted runs no longer hold back the rest (`hot-mirror-unit.test.ts`); after a write-queue barrier times out, run scans go to the in-memory copy for five seconds instead of waiting two seconds per page. The retry, rerun, run and gate routes drop their `RerunError` / `ResumeError` catches (both are `HttpError`s, so `handler()` answers them, now with `code`). Tests pin `exception.fingerprint` on an error log line and on a failed span's `exception` event, and the isolate abort test asserts V8's abort (`SIGABRT` / 134) rather than any failure. Docs: the realm no longer sends untrusted builders to `isolate` (engine.md, the isolate.ts header, the `LANDFLOW_EXPRESSION_SANDBOX` row), engine.md describes Postgres mode's hot copy, development.md lists `postgres-hot-mirror.test.ts`, the 50,000-run measurement is given as a range, CLAUDE.md names `pnpm check`, and the roadmap lists the wave 6 follow-ups. | [roadmap](roadmap.md#backend-hardening-2026-11) |
| (this change) | A domain error thrown by a `singleton()` store answered 500 on a production host: deleting a folder that still held a workflow, after a page had rendered, gave `Internal error` instead of 409. `next build` bundles a module once per layer (route handlers, pages), the `folders` singleton made in the page layer threw that layer's `FolderNotEmptyError`, and the route layer's `handler()` saw no `instanceof HttpError`. `HttpError` now brands its prototype with `Symbol.for('landflow.HttpError')` and overrides `Symbol.hasInstance`: an `HttpError` from any copy matches, and a subclass matches by its literal `errorName` along the error's class chain. `test/error-mapping.test.ts` throws a `FolderNotEmptyError` from a second copy of the module (`vi.resetModules()`) through `handler()`; checked on a production build, where the same request answers 409 with `code: "folder_not_empty_error"` and its `folders` and `workflows` fields. | [constitution](constitution.md) |
| (this change) | Specs for 15 next features in docs/specs/ (written 2026-11-05 on the feature-specs checkout, landed on main 2026-11-14); first slice of composite governance rules: `compositeRules` in the governance document, `lib/server/governance/composite-rules.ts` hooked into `workflowViolations()` through `registerWorkflowRule(name, check)` (a keyed `singleton()` list, so every bundle copy of policy.ts sees the check once; registered at boot by the composition root, runtime.ts), errors refuse publishing and warns don't, `AI_STEP_RULE`; test/composite-rules.test.ts | composites.md#governance-rules |
| (this change) | Chat kit, wave 1 (CH1, CH5, CH6, CH7, CH9, CH10): `chat-motion.tsx` (`ChatMotion`, `ChatList` with Jump to latest, `Collapsible`, `StatusSwap`, `StreamingText`, on lazily loaded `motion`), `ActivityTrace` with `lib/shared/activity.ts`, `ReplyMeta` `info`, `ModelChip` and the composer's `chip`, `ChatError` `actions`, `ShortReply` and `firstSentences`, a `--animate-caret` token; the hosted widget's bubbles rise in and its typing indicator is three dots, both off under Reduce motion. No surface uses the new pieces yet. Tests: `chat-kit.test.ts`, `chat-client.test.ts` | [Chat surfaces](assistant.md#chat-surfaces), [Hosted chat](features/ai.md#hosted-chat-and-widget) |
| (this change) | Personal preferences and the conversations store: a per-person `user_preferences` row (Show developer details, Keep my conversations, briefing delivery) behind `GET/PATCH /api/me/preferences`, and owner-only conversations with their items behind `/api/conversations` (session only, 404 for anyone else's, delete is real, brain links re-checked on read, deleted after `LANDFLOW_CHAT_RETENTION_DAYS` = 90 by a leader service). Client hooks `usePreferences` and `useConversation`, and the history popover (a sheet on a phone) the chats will mount. Migrations 2070 and 2071. Review fixes: a first message over 200 characters no longer fails the create (the title is clipped to 80 before the POST); the history popover loads the subject group with its own request and pages each group alone; the preferences API description says an unknown key is dropped; the preferences cache is cleared at sign-out (a two-line edit to `components/auth/viewer.tsx`, outside the brief's file list); a failed save restores only its key; one `storage` listener serves all subscribers; a create in flight no longer overrides `open` or `startNew`; item size is counted in bytes; conversation search matches non-ASCII words as typed. | [assistant.md](assistant.md#conversation-history) |
| (this change) | The assistant chat server streams and meters. Spotlight's route sends `step.start`, `text.delta` (batches of at most 50 ms), `thinking` (a tool step's text, never the reply), the checked `text` of the last step, timed `tool` events with an id and a short `detail`, and `done` with `costUsd` and `local`; failures are `error` events with a `kind` and, after a tool step, a `checkpoint` that `resume: { checkpoint }` continues from (409 `checkpoint_expired` otherwise; 15 minutes, 200 entries, per user). The Agent architect gets the same steps, timings, errors and `resume`. Both now run in an `assistantScope`, so their model calls write `ai_usage` rows with a `surface` (migration 2072), count in Insights and obey the per-user caps (CH8). The pure guards moved from `chat.ts` to `guards.ts`. Tests: `assistant-stream.test.ts`, `assistant-metering.test.ts`. | [assistant](assistant.md#the-stream) |
| (this change) | Tagging with @, the foundation (B04): `GET /api/mentions` lists what the caller can open per kind (workflows, nodes, runs, agents, teams, data tables, templates, node types, plugins, brain pages, people, credentials; at most 8 each, a prefix match first, then the newest, access checked once per kind); `resolveMentions` turns tags into a block of at most 6 KB (8 tags, 1.5 KB each, every line redacted, no credential value, a missing and a forbidden tag give the same "Couldn't read @Name"); the `MentionPicker` listbox, `MentionChips` and `useMentionPicker` (a popover on a desktop, a sheet above the keyboard on a phone, keys, recents). Not mounted anywhere yet; Spotlight and Ask the brain take it in a later wave. `test/mentions.test.ts` | [assistant](assistant.md#tagging-with-) |
| (this change) | Work events and the person's own briefing (backbone B05): ledger rows can have an owner and a visibility (migration 2073), and a private work event is read by its owner alone, owners and admins included, through every reader (usage routes, Brain page, MCP brain tools, flows) and is left out of the daily rollups; API key scopes `brain:read` and `brain:write` (`brain:write` includes `brain:read`; `POST /api/brain/ask` now needs `brain:read`); `POST /api/brain/work`, `DELETE /api/brain/work/:sourceId`, `GET /api/brain/briefing`; the `work.session` kind; the admin setting `brain.workAutoSend`; the `WorkEventInput` contract in `packages/protocol` | [work-and-briefings.md](work-and-briefings.md) |
| (this change) | Shell: the rail has nine items (Home, Workflows, Runs, Agents, Brain, Data tables, Credentials, Plugins, Settings) and no footer controls; section tabs (`components/shell/section-nav.tsx`, one layout per section) sit above the list pages of Workflows (Templates, Runbooks with the Landfall plugin), Runs (Activity, Insights, and Search until B20 merges the pages) and Plugins (Installed, Marketplace, Build your own), not on the editor, a run's trace or compare; on a phone the strip is a row that scrolls sideways, and the Flows and Runs tabs light for every page of their group; the user menu holds Theme and Build a plugin; the Live dot became a "Reconnecting…" line and the plugin count a red dot when one is offline; the Preview pill is gone from the rail; the switcher has Manage projects and /projects lost its intro; `lookScript` left the root layout; `test/section-nav.test.ts` | [ux-simplification](ux-simplification.md#navigation) |
| (this change) | The Classic look is gone (X5): every screen draws the New look and the look switch, `useLook` and `useNewLook` are removed. Home: the primary button is New workflow, "Open polyglot demo" moved to the empty state, a Needs you tile replaces Plugins online, the Plugins panel and meta line are gone and an offline plugin shows one line, a run of one flow is one row, and a suggestion is a compact card (title, one line of why, the button, ⋯) with Show more | [ui-review.md](ui-review.md#the-look), [ux-simplification.md](ux-simplification.md#home-high-impact-m), [second-brain.md](second-brain.md) |
| (this change) | Backbone wave 1 integration (B01–B07 merged on `backbone/wave-1`): `test/runtime.test.ts` pins the `conversation retention` leader service B02 added after `data maintenance` in the main role's boot order. | [roadmap](roadmap.md) |
| (this change) | Wave 1 review leftovers: tag digests write counts with thousands separators so `redact()` no longer masks a team's budget, a run's tokens or a big table's row count; the old-private-work test now has a real dead-weight case with a control; the rail's "Reconnecting…" shows only after the stream was connected once and dropped (`showReconnecting`), not on every load; four docs that still described the old rail (Runbooks, Marketplace, Insights, the run trace's rail mark) | backbone wave 1 |
| (this change) | Production CSP: the browser no longer builds zod object schemas, which made zod probe `new Function` and report a script-src eval violation on every page. `lib/shared/preferences.ts` (loaded on every page) has no zod; its schemas moved to `preferences-schema.ts` (server and tests), and `test/preferences.test.ts` keeps the two in step. `lib/shared/conversations.ts` sets zod jitless in the browser, as `ui-spec.ts` does. A headed pass over 17 pages at desktop and phone size under the enforced CSP shows 0 violations; the constitution's Headers rule now says how a browser module keeps clear of the probe. | [constitution](constitution.md) |
| (this change) | Editor Spotlight on the chat kit (B08): `components/editor/spotlight.tsx` split into `spotlight/` (index, panel, reply, timeline-view, composer, proposal); its box is the kit's `ChatComposer` with the model chip; its timeline is `ChatList`; replies stream their words, show one activity trace (live line, then *N steps · T s*) and the full footer (model, time, steps, tokens, cost or local), and are cut to two sentences with Show more. The store keeps `steps`, `text`, `thinking`, `meta` and the failed reply's `checkpoint` (`applyStreamEvent`); build steps map to trace rows (`toActivitySteps`). `SpotlightPanel` takes `hidden`. Review fixes: a finished change's trace is closed on desktop too and keeps the stream's timed rows (`mergeTrace` adds only the plan's changes); the Working line stays mounted until the first words so it cross-fades; thinking rows get unique ids; docs/assistant.md and limitations.md match. Round 3: the Working slot stays mounted for the whole live streamed reply (`swapState`, tested) so the first words cross-fade in the builder chat too; `StatusSwap` gained `silent` (an edit to B01's `chat-motion.tsx`); model-call rows read Thinking. | [assistant](assistant.md#the-spotlight-panel) |
| (this change) | Ask the brain (B09): the answer streams (what it found, then the steps and the words, then the model, time and cost), `POST /api/brain/ask` streams when asked with `Accept: text/event-stream` and takes `history` (the last two turns) for follow-ups, a `[n]` with no source is dropped and a source is numbered only when cited, and Ask is metered (surface `brain`). The panel is docked as a column beside the page on a desktop (a bottom sheet on a phone); the brain page has one Ask button and ⌘I instead of four ways in. | [second-brain](second-brain.md#the-brain-page) |
| (this change) | Agent chats on the kit (B10, CH6, CH7, CH13, CH23): the agent test chat, the Agent architect and the analyst use `ActivityTrace`, `ReplyMeta` (with time and cost) and `ChatList`; the test chat has a model chip and **Earlier tests** (the agent's earlier test sessions), and its trace id became the activity line's Open trace link; the architect keeps its conversation (`useConversation`, surface `architect`), reopens the latest under an Earlier divider and offers Continue from step N after a model error (`resume`); Sessions has People / Automations / All (default People), pages with `cursor`, and no Auto-refresh switch; `GET /api/agents/:id/sessions` takes `audience`, `source`, `limit` and `cursor` and answers `nextCursor` (`listSessions` filters in the file and the database store). Tests: `agent-sessions-filter.test.ts` | [agents](agents.md#test-chat), [agent architect](agents.md#agent-architect), [api](reference/api.md) |
| (this change) | Editor layout (B11): the right column holds Spotlight or the flow chat, with a Spotlight | Chat strip when the workflow has a chat trigger (`chatColumnAfter` in `components/editor/chrome.ts`; the one not shown stays mounted); the flow chat is on the chat kit (list, StatusSwap, reply footer, model chip, plain errors); the top bar has seven controls (back, name, save state, Spotlight, Run, Publish, ⋯) and drafts autosave 2 s after the last edit; Publish says "Publish" or "Live v3"; Tags and Version history moved into ⋯, Git history is always there (its dialog says when source control is not set up; saved workflows only); autosaves send `x-landflow-autosave` and the server folds one person's autosaves within 10 minutes into one version (`recordVersion` in `lib/server/versions.ts`: same number, a new id, the old row deleted, and never a version a run, an open publish request, a schedule or the published one names, so four-eyes staleness still fires); the tab-leave save uses `keepalive`; one save at a time; one canvas toolbar with zoom, and the minimap only above 25 nodes; one Import… dialog. | [editor](features/editor.md#the-editors-chrome) |
| (this change) | Show developer details (B12, closes X8): one per-person switch in the user menu hides type ids, transports, languages, run and trace ids, mode chips, the run list's ID and Mode columns, model and tool footers and Debug access until it is on; the user menu also holds Keep my conversations (turning it off offers to delete the kept ones); the inert look toggle is deleted (finishes X5) | [UI review](ui-review.md#developer-details) |
| (this change) | MCP for everyone (B13): an API key sees and calls only the instance MCP tools its scopes cover (`TOOL_SCOPES`); new tools `brain_ask`, `my_briefing` and `record_work` (private work as the key's user); the `morning-briefing` prompt and `brain://briefing/today`, `brain://briefing/yesterday`, `brain://work/today` resources. Review fixes: `record_work` keeps `prs` and checks limits per event through `recordWork`; the key-scope path is tested over HTTP; briefing headings carry dates and a long line drops its link rather than cutting it; docs updated. | [work and briefings](work-and-briefings.md#over-mcp) |
| (this change) | The `landflow-brain` Claude Code mod, first slice (B14): a per-person mod that keeps a local journal of a session in an allowed repository (counts, directories, commits, PRs and a title made by rules on the machine), holds it for review, sends it as a private `work.session` with `/brain review`, and brings your own yesterday into the system prompt at the next session start (2 s, or cached). `/brain today\|review\|status\|pause\|resume\|forget`, a status line, an offline queue (500 events, 14 days, backoff 30 s to 30 min, a 401 stops). Review fixes: recording goes on after `/clear` and an in-process resume; absolute, home and drive paths are dropped from titles; the briefing is always read so an admin's `autoSend: forbidden` and brain-off hold with `sync: false` (an unknown policy holds in mode `auto`); a 401 stop lifts when the key changes; `/brain forget` keeps the entry until the delete is answered; `/brain pause` closes the running entry; session start no longer waits for the flush; the queue is re-read before it settles; refused and rejected events show in `/brain status`; the sent event is stored; `git -C` commits and empty-output or echoed commits are read correctly. Round two: every journal write is serialised so a flush can no longer undo a `sent` mark; titles drop `..` paths and prefixed ones (`@/…`, `--dir=/…`, `file://`); a request that hangs counts as unreachable after 10 s; `/brain resume` says when the brain is off; a forgotten entry waiting for its delete outlives the 14-day horizon and is listed in `/brain status`. | [landflow-brain-mod](landflow-brain-mod.md) |
| (this change) | `landflow work`: the harness-agnostic daily offloader (B15). `work repos add/remove/list` keeps an allow-list; `work collect` reads the day's commit subjects, branches, directories touched and (with `gh`) PRs, never a diff or file, summarises them on the machine without a model into one held entry per repo per day (`work/held.json`, 0600, 500 entries, 14 days); `work review` (`--send`, `--drop`, `--title`), `work send` (asks; `--yes` without a terminal and only where `brain.workAutoSend` allows), `work status`, `work forget`; source `landflow-cli`, `sourceId` `<owner>/<name>@<day>`; the CLI keeps a tested copy of the work-event contract; scheduling examples for cron, launchd and Task Scheduler; `test/work.test.ts`. Review fixes: `collect` gives Git local midnight for a bare day and uses `--branches --remotes` (no stash commits); a re-collect keeps an edited title (`titleEdited`); `review --send` has the same no-terminal guard as `send` (`--yes`, `brain.workAutoSend`, unreadable setting refuses); a real-git test. | [cli](features/cli.md#recording-your-day-landflow-work), [work-and-briefings](work-and-briefings.md) |
| (this change) | Wave 1 carryover: `ChatList`'s Jump to latest glide ends on `scrollend` or after a second (older Safari has no `scrollend`, and the list stopped following), and a reply that grew during the glide is scrolled to its new bottom when the glide ends. | [Chat surfaces](assistant.md#chat-surfaces) |
| (this change) | Wave 1 carryover, conversations: a brain conversation's tags survive a read (the sanitizer checks `mentions` refs the way the @ picker does: brain pages, people, teams, nodes, runs, templates, node types and plugins, where it used to keep only resource kinds and entities); `conversationMetaSchema` keeps `local`, and the Agent architect saves and restores it, so a reopened local reply still says *local*; titles clip by code point; the history popover takes `ownSurfaceOnly` and the architect lists only architect conversations; the retention service test now drives the timer (it also showed the service is already running in a test process). Tests: `conversations.test.ts`, `agent-sessions-filter.test.ts` | [assistant.md](assistant.md#conversation-history), [agents](agents.md#agent-architect) |
| (this change) | Wave 1 carryover, privacy and work events: `deleteUser` also removes the person's conversations (with their items), preferences and private work events; `POST /api/brain/work` (and MCP `record_work`) refuses an `occurredAt` more than a day ahead; a test keeps `WORK_FIELDS` and the declarable kinds in step with `deploy/brain/work-kinds.json`. Tests: `brain-work.test.ts` | [work and briefings](work-and-briefings.md#the-fields-and-limits), [assistant.md](assistant.md#conversation-history) |
| (this change) | Wave 1 carryover, assistant server: `error.kind` is required in `ChatEvent` and the architect's stream events (both routes always sent it); a stalled reply's `error.step` is the step a resume runs next, as a model error's is, so *Continue from step N* is right for both; Spotlight's and Ask the brain's provisional words go through `provisionalText` (`lib/shared/leaked-markup.ts`, which the server guard now shares), so a local model's `<tool_call>`, `<function=…>` and `<think>` text never shows while it streams; `POST /api/agents/design` answers 429 with the cap's message when the caller's AI usage cap is reached, instead of an empty draft; tests for the request span's `landflow.error.kind` and for a member's Spotlight call through the route on a workflow they cannot read; `docs/observability.md` lists `architect` and `landflow.error.kind`; the two AI usage paragraphs of `docs/assistant.md` are one; the `/api/brain/ask` API row and OpenAPI description say it needs `brain:read`. Tests: `assistant-stream.test.ts`, `assistant-metering.test.ts`, `agent-design.test.ts`, `spotlight-fluency.test.ts` | [assistant.md](assistant.md#the-stream), [observability](observability.md), [api](reference/api.md) |
| (this change) | Wave 1 carryover, tagging: a person's digest reads their brain page with the caller as the viewer (as a brain page's digest does); a person without a name is called by their email's local part in both the picker and the digest (`personName`), never `Person “”`; the digest test now reads a real agent (and refuses another project's), a team and a plugin; `docs/limitations.md` says what `redact()` masks in a digest. Tests: `mentions.test.ts` | [assistant.md](assistant.md#tagging-with-) |
| (this change) | Wave 1 carryover, shell and Home: the folded rail shows an amber dot while reconnecting (the words hide with the labels); the project switcher always separates Manage projects from the last project; `useNeedsYou` is one shared list (Home, the rail and the runs page make one `/api/runs/needs-you` request per tick) and refreshes on `execution.paused` too; the Needs you tile no longer draws its number in red; a suggestion card keeps its *Leaves …* warnings in view when folded, and `cardButtons` (Apply or the advice's first button, the rest under ⋯) is tested; Recent executions says when every listed run is still going; the Classic-removal leftovers are gone (stray braces and fragment, "new look" comments, a `classic` local). `docs/second-brain.md` says where a flow's buttons sit on the card. Tests: `home.test.ts` | [ux-simplification](ux-simplification.md), [second brain](second-brain.md) |
| (this change) | Wave 1 carryover, what waits: `docs/roadmap.md` lists the review notes left open (footer token wording, Insights per workflow vs today, checkpoints taken before the stream, the section tabs' request and roles, the switcher's two `/projects` items), each with its reason. | [roadmap](roadmap.md) |
| (this change) | Wave 2 review leftovers: autosave's "is this version referenced" check asks by workflow and version, so it uses the `executions_workflow` index instead of scanning every run on each autosave (B11); the Marketplace Explore tiles hide their language and runtime badges unless **Show developer details** is on (B12, X8); the editor, assistant and UI review pages and the version history header say what autosave, Tags… and Import… do now (B11); the Working line swaps to the reply at once under Reduce motion (MotionConfig stops transforms, not opacity), as docs/assistant.md says (B08). | [editor](features/editor.md) |
| (this change) | landflow-brain mod, two privacy fixes: review's **Drop** takes the entry's event out of the offline queue (before, a dropped entry still went at the next flush and then showed nowhere), and an entry a flush sent while the question was open is deleted in the brain as `/brain forget` does; Drop and forget read the journal and the queue in one lock, and an event in a POST not yet answered counts as sent. A title drops a machine path with a space in it: a quoted span (`"…"`, `'…'`, `` `…` ``, `“…”`) that holds one, a `\ `-escaped space, and the slashed words after an unquoted one (`~/Library/Application Support/Acme/x.db`). | [landflow-brain mod](landflow-brain-mod.md) |
| (this change) | Ask the brain leftovers (B09): the brain index points at **Ask** instead of a Spotlight bar that is gone; the OpenAPI `askBrain` entry lists `history`, `links`, `cited` and the streamed form; docs/assistant.md names the `brain` usage surface and Ask the brain. | [second brain](second-brain.md) |
| (this change) | landflow-brain mod, titles: after an unquoted machine path that does not end in a file name, the rest of its clause is dropped too (up to `.`, `,`, `;`, `:`, `!`, `?` or a new line), so `~/a/My Big Project/x` leaves no directory; with nothing left the title is "Coding session". Privacy over title quality: `--dir=/Users/alice/x now` now gives `Run with`. | [landflow-brain mod](landflow-brain-mod.md) |
| (this change) | Work events: the host refuses a title or summary that holds a path on the sender's machine (`containsMachinePath` in `@landflow/protocol`: home and volume directories, `~/`, a Windows drive, a UNC share, `file://`; a URL path such as `/api/brain/work` passes). The mod and `landflow work` already clean titles on the machine; this is the backstop for any MCP client or script, found in the wave 2 live check where `record_work` stored `/Users/…` in a title. The CLI's copy of the contract is synced. | [work and briefings](work-and-briefings.md) |
| (this change) | `landflow work` counts read "0 entries" and "1 entry", not "0 entrys" (`plural` gives -ies after a consonant and y), found in the wave 2 live run. | [cli](features/cli.md) |
| (this change) | Spotlight history and @ tags (B16, closes CH12 and CH14): Spotlight keeps its conversation per workflow and brings the last one back under "Earlier" (dimmed) when the editor opens; the clock beside New conversation opens the history; a proposal still waiting is dry-run against the workflow and reads "This no longer fits the workflow" (no Apply) when it fails. `@` in the composer is the app-wide picker (canvas nodes first, chips above the box); the chat body's `mentions` is `{ refs }` (the old `{ nodes, types }` still works for one release) and the server reads each tag as the caller, puts the digests in the prompt as data and streams one read step per tag; a tag to something forbidden reads "Couldn't read @Name". The unchecked node mention path is gone. | [Spotlight](spotlight.md), [assistant](assistant.md#conversation-history) |
| (this change) | Ask the brain keeps conversations and takes tags (B17): a conversation for each page, the last one back under **Earlier**, the history button in the panel (the row above the box on a phone), `@` in the box tagging pages, people, workflows, runs and more. `POST /api/brain/ask` takes `mentions: { refs }` (at most 8; `about` counts as one `brainPage` tag); each tag is checked as the caller and read into the prompt, and told as a step (`Read @Billing`, or `Couldn't read @Billing` for one that is missing or not theirs, with nothing else). A kept turn stores names, never the block; on reopen a link the reader can no longer open is dropped and the turn says "1 link hidden". Review fixes: an API key tags only what its scopes cover (`lib/server/mentions/key-scopes.ts`), `{ question, about }` keeps its JSON contract (facts), following a link keeps this visit's answers on screen and stores a running turn where it began, tags cut by the cap say "Left out", the history chip reads "Page". | [second brain](second-brain.md#the-brain-page) |
| (this change) | Brain colleagues (B18): a work item can be `team` (read by people who share a project with its owner) or `org` (everyone), set when sent or with `PATCH /api/brain/work/:sourceId`; `POST /api/brain/work/:sourceId/share` posts it as one linked `work.self_update`; the briefing's `related` lists colleagues' team and org work of the last two days with the repo, initiative, customer, area or similar-summary reason; the first `team` or `org` event naming a repo makes a `system` entity (a private one makes none); a person page is linked to its user by email (migration 2080, `personForUser`). | [work-and-briefings](work-and-briefings.md) |
| (this change) | Connect your harness: `/connect` (in the user menu) makes a brain key and shows copy-paste setup for Claude Code (with the landflow-brain mod), Cursor, Codex CLI, Gemini CLI, VS Code and any MCP client, with a Test connection button. Settings → MCP is four collapsed sections (Access token, Connected clients, Tools with each tool's scope, Gateway), split into `mcp-*.tsx` files. Owners and admins get **Allow automatic sending** (`brain.workAutoSend`) there. The api-keys route's scope rules moved into `scopeRefusal`. | [connect your harness](connect-your-harness.md) |
| (this change) | Runs: Executions, Activity and Search runs are one list at `/runs` (tab **All runs**). One search box with Status and Workflow chips and More filters; repeated failures fold into one expandable row; two words for running again (Retry from failed step, Re-run) with the workflow choices one step in; no ID or Mode column (Copy id is in the ⋯); Stop all only while a run runs; tick two rows and Compare 2. `/executions` redirects with its query (`?id=` to `/runs/<id>`), and a compare without two runs goes back to the list. `RETRY_LABEL` and `RERUN_WORD` are in `meta.tsx`. | [Runs list](run-trace.md#the-runs-list) |
| (this change) | Run trace (B21): a tab shows only with something in it (Story and Trace always, Team with more than one taking part, Artifacts with something made); the Report tab is the Story's summary (tiles that read 0 and an empty "next" are left out, the rest folds under **The report in full**); no time slider on a run under 5 s or with one taking part; the status is said once in the header (status, duration, when); **Debug access** moved into the header's **⋯** and its scopes read in plain words, tool names behind Show developer details. Closes P-trace-1 to P-trace-5 and X13 for runs. | [run-trace](run-trace.md) |
| (this change) | Workflow runs and tests (B22): a workflow's tabs are Editor \| Runs \| Tests at `/workflows/:id/runs` and `/tests` (the old `executions` and `evaluations` links redirect, keeping the query). The run view drops the Canvas / Run trace switch (Full screen opens the trace) and the id, hash and mode chip from its header (Copy run id is in ⋯); Debug in editor moves into the Re-run menu, with "Retry from failed step" and "Re-run". Auto refresh is gone: the list updates live and waits while the pointer is over it or it is scrolled down. Tests loses the yellow warning box and says "Add an evaluation trigger". | [editor](features/editor.md) |
| (this change) | Node panel and editor details (B23): the header is the name, one line of description and one Run step (Breakpoint and Run from here lead Settings, the empty Output pane has no second Run step); Settings shows On error, Retry on fail and Notes with the rest under More, descriptions on one line; the Schema / Table / JSON switch shows only with data; Events is a tab inside the open logs panel; workflow settings show Error workflow, Notify on failure and Time zone with the rest under Advanced; a node's toolbar is Run, Disable, Delete and ⋯ and the right-click menu matches. Closes P-np-1 to 4 and P-ed-5 to 7. | [editor](features/editor.md) |
| (this change) | Wave 2 carryover, `landflow work` (B15): commit subjects in a title are joined with " · " instead of "; " (a semicolon is one of the two signs `looksLikeCode` needs, so a long title with "return early" or "let users" fell back to the counts); a dropped day stays dropped when it is collected again; `collect` reports only the days it kept and notes those older than 14 days; the real-git test turns off commit signing and the tests pin the date. | [cli](features/cli.md#recording-your-day-landflow-work) |
| (this change) | Wave 2 carryover, the landflow-brain mod (B14): a title drops a word that holds a flag path (`-I/usr/local/include`) or a machine's top-level directory (`/usr/`, `/Users/`, `/home/`…) anywhere in it, and a tagged file (`@src/app/page.tsx`) reduces to `src/app` without the `@`; a refused batch's `problems` note is written inside the journal lock with `markSent`'s; the mod page says an entry reads `held` while its event waits in the queue and that Drop takes it out. | [landflow-brain mod](landflow-brain-mod.md) |
| (this change) | Wave 2 carryover, Ask the brain (B09) and the chat kit (B08): a link to a page that does not exist becomes a word whatever its case (`[[Support/11]]` read raw before); a fact about something unnamed reaches the model as "a workflow", not its id, and the prompt asks for no ids; the writing step's tool event is `write_answer`, not a second `read_work`; a model that answers JSON streams its words, not `{"answer": "`; leaving /brain mid-answer aborts the request; a usage cap is tested to end as a `cap` error. The kit's short reply counts a list without full stops as one sentence where it ends, so "one sentence, a list, a question" gets Show more, and Show more keeps a 44 px target on a phone without opening a gap above the last line. | [second brain](second-brain.md), [assistant](assistant.md#chat-surfaces) |
| (this change) | Wave 2 carryover, the editor (B11): a failed save's toast has **Retry** (a phone has no Save or ⌘S); a save that answers after the editor closed no longer marks a reopened copy of the same workflow as saved (a per-mount guard), so its next autosave cannot overwrite the flushed edit; the leave flush's comment says it is queued behind a save in flight. editor.md says the leave prompt shows for any unsaved draft and that Copy to editor autosaves its pins; mobile.md's editor header matches the phone (no Save or "Not published" chip; Runs and Tests); the roadmap says why Git history shows for everyone (half of P-ed-3 dropped on purpose). | [editor](features/editor.md#the-editors-chrome) |
| (this change) | Wave 2 carryover, agents, developer details and MCP (B10, B12, B13): the test chat's Earlier tests no longer says the history is yours (it lists the agent's test sessions); a session load still in flight when a message is sent or the chat is reset is dropped (`useAgentRun`), and the Sessions tab drops a page that answers for an audience no longer shown; the `developer-details` imports sit after each file's header comment, and the node tile checks the switch once; `my_briefing` and the `morning-briefing` prompt refuse a day not on the calendar (`2026-13-45`, `2026-02-30`). Docs: the MCP key-scope limitation says the gateway never passes an API key on, its heading drops the package id, the plugin card's endpoints and transport policy are named as behind the switch, deleting a person leaves what was made from their work earlier, `LANDFLOW_CLAUDE_PLUGINS_DIR` names the landflow-brain mod too, and the roadmap's CH13 link and `look-toggle.tsx` lines are right. | [work and briefings](work-and-briefings.md#over-mcp), [agents](agents.md#test-chat), [limitations](limitations.md) |
| (this change) | Wave 2 carryover, the activity trace (B08): the live line's step number counts the rows so far, so it never goes down (it read "step 17", then "Thinking · step 16") and ends at the closed trace's count. | [assistant](assistant.md#chat-surfaces) |
| (this change) | Wave 2 carryover, what waits: `docs/roadmap.md` lists the wave 2 review notes left open (Spotlight changing a flow when asked a question, Ask's long answer, the streaming caret, the footer wrap, the architect's repeated turns, agent sessions at scale, the test chat's child-run link, folded-away version ids, tests not written, MCP internals, developer details vs the footer, Needs you after sign-out, Ask's follow-up heuristic), each with its reason. | [roadmap](roadmap.md) |
| (this change) | Wave 3 integration, MCP briefing (B13 × B18): `my_briefing` leaves the briefing's `related` out of its JSON, so colleagues' shared `team`/`org` work (their titles and summaries) never reaches a person's harness labelled as their own; the tool, the `morning-briefing` prompt and the `brain://` resources carry the caller's own `today` and `yesterday` only. A test has a teammate's `team` item on the same repo that the briefing relates and the tool, prompt and resources leave out. | [work and briefings](work-and-briefings.md#over-mcp), [AI](features/ai.md#mcp) |
| (this change) | `ai.call` usage events carry the prompt-cache counts: `attrs.cacheRead` and `attrs.cacheWrite` (parts of `attrs.input`, which is the whole prompt; 0 when the provider reports none), so a cost ledger reading `GET /api/usage/events?kinds=ai.call` can price cache reads and writes apart. Asked for by Landledger's ingest. | [second brain](second-brain.md) |
| (this change) | Wave 3 review leftovers: one key-scope table for @ tags (`MENTION_KEY_SCOPE`), read by `/api/mentions`, Spotlight and Ask the brain, with each kind's own route scopes (people and credentials `admin`, runs `executions:read`, node types, plugins and the rest `workflows:read`, a brain page `brain:read` or `workflows:read`), so a `brain:read` key no longer reads a person's name and role through Ask; Spotlight reports a tag its key may not read as "Couldn't read @X" as Ask does; a chosen tag leaves one space, and canvas reads match on their own ref. On a phone a run's MCP caller, automatic retry time and buffered mark are lines in **⋯**. Links go straight to `/workflows/:id/runs[/:id]` and `/runs` (alerts, MCP and error-workflow URLs too), and the UI says Runs (Home's Recent runs and Runs (24h), the Run Trace's back link, the proposal toast, the editor's Open in Runs, Runs to keep). OpenAPI `askBrain` has `mentions` and `tags`. A work event's visibility change to or from `org` recomputes that day's `usage_daily`; a colleague's item posted as an update is related once. Settings → MCP polls approvals once; Connect's test result wraps. Docs: tags in Spotlight and Ask (B16, B17), Debug access in ⋯ (B21), shared harness events (B18), repository pages from `team`/`org` work. | [assistant](assistant.md#tagging-with-), [work and briefings](work-and-briefings.md), [editor](features/editor.md) |
| (this change) | Choosing an @ tag turns the typed `@query` into `@Name` in the box (`replaceMentionText`), so the message still reads as a sentence in the chat and in history ("What does @Sample ticket do?" instead of "What does do?"); the chip is still what the server reads. Found in the wave 3 live check. | [assistant](assistant.md) |
| (this change) | Spotlight motion and states (B24): a desktop opens Spotlight folded (kept per browser); the fold eases its width and the canvas fits once after it; Apply folds the card into its row while the ghosts fade into the real nodes, and Undo plays it back; errors have actions (Continue from step N, Open AI caps for admins, Open the node, Ask Spotlight to fix it); the header has Asks first \| Autonomous with a one-time note; the empty panel has one line (the composer's bubbles are the starters); Continue keeps the failed reply's changes and a retried reply auto-applies and tests in Autonomous mode; changes applied together share one Undo; the phone sheet's three heights ease and follow the drag; a phone's folded bar has Review and Apply when a change waits | [assistant](assistant.md#fold-apply-and-errors) |
| (this change) | B25, the Agents list and persona, plainer: the persona strip shows only with no agents; a filter with no agents behind it is hidden (and the row with it when only All is left; Group by shows only once an agent has a team); cards show the name, what it does and the status, with no model, tools, sessions, channels or tone words (they stay on the agent's page), and team cards the name, goal and status; **Import JSON** moved from the header into New agent as a third way in, **From a file**, beside the gallery and Design with AI; the Persona tab has one **What it does** field, the description and What I do folded under **Card text (optional)**, and the system prompt behind **View prompt** (read-only; **Edit** jumps to the instructions). | [agents](agents.md#the-roster) |
| (this change) | Agent detail has three tabs, Persona, Abilities and Activity. | (this change) | Settings in five groups (Workspace, People & access, Governance, AI, Infrastructure); API docs moved to the Developers page (`#api` and `?section=api` go there); the built-in price table folded behind "Show built-in prices" and AI caps their own section; Security, Workers and Transports show status rows ("SSO: not configured · How to set up ›") and one banner for identical errors; Audit has Log and Configuration tabs and its filters apply on change; General keeps json-render under Advanced and "Prune now" in the ⋯ menu; one sentence of help plus a "?" help-tip across the settings | [settings and data](features/settings-and-data.md) |

| (this change) | The landflow-brain mod's colleagues (B29, mod 0.2.0): `/brain sync` lists yesterday and the related colleagues with each reason; `/brain` opens a pane (Today, Yesterday, Related colleagues as cards, Esc closes it, never opened on its own); a one-line band appears only when a colleague touched the same thing, dismissable for the day; `/brain review` has Send wider (team or org; private stays the default) and `/brain visibility <id> private\|team\|org` changes a sent item with PATCH; Post as my update (`/brain post <id>`, a pane button) asks first; when the admin forbids automatic sending, mode `auto` acts as `review` and the status line says "brain: review (auto off by admin)". Review fixes: Post as my update sends the item's source, an earlier day's view is never drawn, a visibility change waits for a POST in flight and updates `/brain status`. | [landflow-brain mod](landflow-brain-mod.md#colleagues) |
| (this change) | Wave 3 review leftovers, fixed in wave 4's carryover. Runs: an open failure group stays open when its oldest run is deleted, paged out or filtered out (the list holds open groups by `failureKey`). Run Trace: the timeline card above the tabs is decided from the whole run, so a replay no longer shifts the tabs; a passing run with nothing reported and no checks draws no empty summary grid. Work: teammates are found by `projects.type`, not the `personal_` id prefix; related colleagues and entity rows look a user up once; a test shows a `team` item on its owner's person page for a teammate only. Settings → MCP's Tools sentence names the approvals. | (this change) | Tomorrow sync (B30): the template `brain-tomorrow-sync` runs at 02:00 in the instance time zone through five brain nodes (List People, Gather Work, Write Briefing, Save Briefing, Deliver Briefing) and keeps one `ready` briefing per person and day in `brain_briefings` (migration 2090; run data holds ids and counts only), with a plain-text fallback when the brain model is off; `briefingFor` and the MCP `morning-briefing` serve it (`source: flow`, "from last night's sync"); delivery by Slack or email only when the person chose it; `LANDFLOW_BRIEFING_MAX_CHARS`. Review fixes: the sync's default day is the day it is read on (a 02:00 run gathers yesterday and today so far); Gather, Write, Save and Deliver run only in an owner's or admin's workflow; the sync text reaches MCP only through the quoted `morning-briefing` prompt (not `my_briefing` or the resources); a bad item is skipped with a warning; the model call times out per person; the mod showing the text is carried to B38. | [work-and-briefings](work-and-briefings.md#the-nightly-sync) |
| (this change) | GitHub into the brain (B31): the **GitHub to Work Event** node turns a GitHub Webhook Trigger delivery (pull request opened, merged, closed or review requested, a review's state, a CI run or commit status) into one work event with a stable id, the `work-github` template records it and keeps the repository as a system, and five new kinds (`work.pr_closed`, `work.pr_reviewed`, `work.review_requested`, `work.ci_failed`, `work.ci_passed`) are declared. No diffs, code or review text are kept; **People** maps logins to emails. The declare script lets a kind list its own fields, because a kind holds at most 20 | [integrations](features/integrations.md#github-into-the-brain) |

| (this change) | Plugins, Marketplace and Templates (B33): Plugins is one column with the discovery feed behind **Show feed**, **Add endpoint** inside the Scan button's menu, only the list count in the header and 12 node chips with **+N** per card; Marketplace has Explore (plugins, agents, skills and composites; no workflows, a link to Templates; one category dropdown; a source dropdown only with several marketplaces) and Installed (plugins and skills with a type filter); Templates lost its project menu and Import button, lists usable templates first and dims the rest with *Needs <plugin>* | [marketplace.md](marketplace.md#using-it), [plugins.md](plugins.md), [features/editor.md](features/editor.md) |
| (this change) | Backbone wave 5, B34: the Workflows page has one New menu (Workflow, From template, Import…, Folder) and rows show the last run instead of the step count; Credentials has no type grid and its picker groups by app without "API" suffixes; a data table has Import and Export CSV in ⋯ and a + on the header row for a new column; a project has four tabs (Overview, Members, Settings with Variables, Audit log) and old `?tab=` links map to them; a page's header button hides while its empty state has its own; the sign-in page has no welcome sentence and an invalid invite shows only its message. Review fixes: the project Overview has a New menu and lists its data tables and agents with Move to project; the phone row shows the last-run badge; empty Workflows states carry Folder and Import; last runs load after the list; the Add-column + sticks to the right; X10 is closed only for Workflows, Credentials and table rows. | [roadmap](roadmap.md#lists-credentials-tables-projects-and-sign-in-b34-backbone-wave-5), [ui-review.md](ui-review.md) |

| (this change) | Wave 5 integration, B33 x B34: Workflows' **New → Import…** takes a template file again (the Templates page's Import button left in B33): a file whose `kind` is `landflow/template` goes to `POST /api/templates` through `useTemplateImport` (now with `importText`), in the folder's project (else the project picked), and the template's page opens; any other file still goes to the workflow importer. The "no UI for template-file Import" limitation is gone. | [features/editor.md](features/editor.md), [roadmap](roadmap.md#lists-credentials-tables-projects-and-sign-in-b34-backbone-wave-5) |
| (this change) | Test fix: the MCP brain tests recorded work "an hour ago", which falls on yesterday during the first hour after local midnight, so four tests failed then; they now record it a second ago | [work and briefings](work-and-briefings.md) |
| (this change) | Wave 5 fix, the Tomorrow sync's privacy (B30): the runner records `startedBy` for every run a person starts (manual, chat, MCP, agent tool, re-run, a retry they ask for, a sub-workflow of one; an automatic retry keeps its run's starter and is marked `retryAutomatic`), and the five briefing nodes allow a run only when an owner or admin started it, or when a trigger started it (not a re-run, not a manual retry) on the workflow's current saved or published version, never an older one from its history. Closes a member's MCP run from Gather Work with someone else's `userId`, a member's re-run of the nightly run with an older graph, and a member's plain re-run that sent everyone's briefing again; Gather, Write, Save and Deliver also do nothing with the brain off, and a member's save makes the run fail with a message that says an owner or admin must save it again. Tested through the real runner | [work and briefings](work-and-briefings.md#the-nightly-sync) |
